> **⛔ c1 GATE-0 NO-GO 13-08-2026 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5`) — warm-up killed at the ceiling,
> 0 output bytes; the lane did NOT open.** First probe after the 13-08 blanket spend
> authorization, and the first aimed at **c1** (this month's operating profile) rather than
> c4. `elapsed_ms` **300 198** against `HARD_TIMEOUT_MS` 300 000 — the **our-kill** signature
> (ceiling + ~198 ms teardown), **not** a route latency reading; measured leg never ran
> (fail-closed stop). Raw envelope captured (H2326's fix is live) and it records **`bytes=1`,
> `matched=-`** — no `429`/rate-limit/usage-limit string, no API error text, no reset time. So
> this is a **different class** from c1's only other reading (26-07: `rate_limit`, 6 424 ms,
> 822 B). **c1 has two readings and zero PASSes, ever.**
> **Cost UNKNOWN, not zero** — `cost_evaluable: false`, `unevaluable_calls: 1`; a `0` under a
> false `cost_evaluable` means *not evaluable*, and a killed call still bills. Ledger clean:
> `calls_spent: 1`, `finalized: true`, `pending_calls: 0`.
> **Do NOT re-derive the ceiling for this shape** (H2299): a 0-byte kill gives no number to fit.
> **Confound the c4 series never had:** the probe was fired from a live session running under
> **the same `claude1` profile it probed** — one `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` shared by parent and
> child. That is H2326 hypothesis 3 (self-contention with the driving session), here
> structurally true rather than speculative. It does not retro-explain the c4 NO-GO days
> (those targeted a profile the driver was not using), but it weakens this single reading as
> evidence about the *route*.
> **Ration:** attempt 1 of 2 for 13-08 UTC; next legal c1 probe **≥ 18:40:43 UTC** (six hours
> from the `probe_call` ROW at `12:40:43.525Z`, never the run-id start — Uprava FINDINGS §319).
> **No reroll fired** — a NO-GO means STOP, and a sibling-profile probe in the same sitting is
> a reroll wearing a different flag.
> **Next:** [H2647](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/handoffs/H2647-Opus_RussianTranslation_c1-gate0-warmup-hang-selfcontention-discriminate_13.08.26.md)

> **H2591 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5`) — Run bounded Claude comparison on sealed Flash PREP
> contexts — 🔴 EXECUTED 12-08-2026, verdict ❌ INCONCLUSIVE.** A human authorized the spend
> with billing classified UNKNOWN; all eight `--check` conditions passed with **0 transport
> calls**; the sealed order ran to completion — **16/16 reserved calls, no stop, every
> reservation finalized exactly once**. Arms tied at **4/8 audited cards each**
> (A 2 092 s, B 1 529 s). **No promotion, no store/TM write.**
> **The GO was rejected on inspection, and that is the result.** The rule fired GO on a
> +26.9 % wall margin, but arm A's three failed calls burned 967 s against arm B's 625 s —
> the margin is mostly the difference between how long each arm took to *fail*. On the four
> cards both arms returned schema for it falls to +20.9 % across two opposite signs (n=4).
> **The token axis does not exist: 7 of 16 calls returned every usage counter zeroed**,
> including two arm-A calls that produced cards passing the audit at coverage 1.0.
> `usage_evaluable()` checked the SHAPE of the usage block, not whether it said anything, so
> a zero-filled dict passed as present usage — worse than absent, because it silently
> deflates whichever arm receives it. The run should have stopped at call 1. Fixed in the
> same pass (all-zero usage now stops the run; any usage hole or unattested model forces
> INCONCLUSIVE ahead of the GO arithmetic), pinned by a new case — matrix 13/13, window
> suite 211/211.
> **What the run did establish:** the markup-heavy stratum is unqualifiable at this call
> shape (`Srama`/`samIpa`, 234 placeholders each, returned non-JSON at char 0 in BOTH arms,
> 141–324 s — the card defeats the whole-card call regardless of context, which is a
> statement about production's presplit lane); the two asymmetric pairs cancel
> (`vyavasTA` passed only in B, `SudDi` only in A, near-identical wall times — the
> silent-empty failures look stochastic); and `rAtra` arm A emitted `{T2}`, a placeholder
> the source never contained, exactly the class the deterministic gate exists for.
> **Residual defects:** envelopes discard the raw result text (calls 5–9/11's output is
> unrecoverable), and an absent `returned_model` does not stop the run (call 09 is a paid,
> unattested call). Receipt: [comparison_receipt.json](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h1210/h2591/comparison_receipt.json) ·
> evidence: [h2591/README.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h1210/h2591/README.md)

> **H2539 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5`) — Run the attested router.cheap two-ticket live canary at
> v1.144.28 and mint gateway-w1 only on GO — 🔴 EXECUTED 10-08-2026, verdict ❌ NO-GO.**
> **No `gateway-w1` production handoff was minted.** Exactly 2 reservations, exactly 2
> dispatched Agent calls, both finalized, `pending_calls: 0`. Ticket 1 sealed
> `schema_compliant: true`; **Ticket 2 sealed `schema_compliant: false`
> (`failure_class: malformed_output`)**, so the GO criteria fail. Both tickets attested
> `model_matches_request: true` on served model `claude-opus-5` — no substitution.
> **Cause was a contradiction between two artifacts this session authored, not a route or
> model defect:** the T2 prompt said "reproduce the German skeleton line EXACTLY", the model
> did (`— 1〉 {%eine Schildkröte%}.`), but the frozen schema pinned the gloss alone
> (`{%eine Schildkröte%}.`). Translation itself was clean — 3/3 senses
> (черепаха / небольшая рыба / водное растение), **0 other deterministic defects** across all
> 13 enumerated classes. **Two residuals a re-qualification must carry:** (1) the 24-case
> schema selftest missed the divergence because every fixture was built from the schema's own
> constants — a selftest must include an instance built from the prompt's inlined source text;
> (2) `gateway_attestation.py` defaults to `isSidechain: true`, but this harness records Agent
> calls as **main turns** (101 main / 0 sidechain), so the default filter yields
> `model_matches_request: null` — both attestations needed `--include-main-turns`, which
> attests the served model for *the window*, not provably for *the single dispatch inside it*.
> Usage/cost stayed `cost_evaluable: false` (`usage_absent_or_malformed`); the ledger's `0`
> cost floor must not be read as "free". All artifacts `synthetic_control` / `promotable: false`.
> Report: [CANARY_QUALIFICATION_REPORT_ROUTER_CHEAP_NO-GO_10-08-2026.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/pwg_ru/h2539/CANARY_QUALIFICATION_REPORT_ROUTER_CHEAP_NO-GO_10-08-2026.md)

> **H2254 🔴 EXECUTED 07-08-2026 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5`), isolated worktree — bounded
> 300 s ceiling converged; the authorized live proof deliberately NOT fired.**
> **Zero model calls, $0.00; the three-call / $3.00 reservation is unspent and intact.**
> Predecessors verified merged on `origin/master` first ([H2253](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/1175),
> [H2173](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/1083)) and their work
> consumed rather than rebuilt — the handoff's 03-08 audit snapshot was materially stale
> (A3 and most of A4 were already FIXED by H2173; classifying before editing is what kept
> this from duplicating merged work).
> **Landed:** one imported `execution_contract.PRODUCTION_HARD_TIMEOUT_MS` replacing two
> copied literals; a request **above** the maximum now REFUSED pre-spawn (operator
> `--timeout` *and* sealed `budgets.timeout_ceil_ms`, at `validate_manifest` +
> `HeadlessEngine.__init__` + the parser default) instead of silently clamped; the retired
> 7 200 s default gone from all three routes; `wrapper_timeout_s()` giving supervisors 120 s
> over the per-call ceiling (a regression this same change would otherwise have introduced —
> equal inner/outer bounds kill the worker during teardown and lose the status file); an
> additive `evidence` block on `canary_gate` receipts (calls, cost + `cost_evaluable`, worst
> wall/route latency, kill-switch state, commit, hashes, artifact paths — absent inputs
> recorded as `null`, never `0`); two doc-truth fixes, one of them an inversion
> (`/pwg-bounded-run` said its `--timeout` was a whole-job budget; it is the per-call ceiling).
> **Gates:** `window_selftest` **210/210**, `pytest tests` **123 passed**, `lang_parity_check`
> 93 entries no drift (35 re-derived, SHARED stands — 0 language-keyed
> tokens in every pipeline file; the only matches are the re-stamp driver naming the tokens
> it greps for), `ci_gate_runner` green. One PRE-EXISTING flaky depth-N spawn failure in
> `headless_worker_selftest`, reproduced on unmodified `origin/master`, not a regression here.
> **Section B — `BLOCKED_ON_LANE_STOP`, and this is the finding, not a shortfall:** the live
> authorization was written 03-08 against a lane with ONE NO-GO day. There are now **three
> consecutive** (03-08 route stall · 05-08 our own kill at the ceiling · 06-08 up-front
> `rate_limit` refusal, $0.00), and `/pwg-live-gate`'s retry policy says that after three the
> lane **stops** and routes to ceiling re-derivation "rather than to another probe". The
> re-derivation must not fire either — it answers a *distribution* problem, and 05-08 gave no
> number to fit while 06-08 came back 4.3× under the ceiling. Diagnosis stays with **H2299**;
> re-authorizing a live sequence is a decision a human should take, after it lands.

> **H2192 🔴 EXECUTED 03-08-2026 (Opus 5 1M `claude-opus-5[1m]`), isolated worktree —
> W1-RV residual: `added_by_one` fires 0/12,000 because it and `omitted_by_one` are ONE
> undirected class.** Prerequisite check first: H1843 and H1844 are both ✅ on origin
> (PRs [#866](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/866),
> [#867](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/867),
> [#872](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/872) merged), so per the
> residual IMPLEMENTATION this took the first open unit needing no human vote — the defect
> the decisions log itself named "the first thing the step-8 gate should be read against".
> **Zero model calls, $0.00**; new
> [`src/rv_added_by_one_diagnosis.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/rv_added_by_one_diagnosis.py)
> derives every number from committed data.
> **Cause:** the two asymmetric labels are converse readings of one configuration (material
> present on one side, absent on the other), the pair key is unordered, and the model reply
> shape was `{"class", "why"}` with no direction field — so one name absorbed the whole event
> class. The *deterministic* arm always emitted `missing_side`, which is the sharpest evidence
> that direction was expressible all along and was dropped in exactly the arm that cannot
> recover it. **Second defect found in passing:** `COARSE_MAP` split the converse pair across
> `divergence`/`omission`, so the K3 projection moved under a vacuous relabelling — recomputed
> on the three committed spike arms, κ is bit-identical under both maps (0.235 / 0.350 /
> 0.216), so **H1901's published kappas need no caveat** and are independently reproduced.
> **Correction to the record:** H1844 and H1901 both blamed Griffith's supplied material;
> measured, Griffith is the *least*-marked witness (0.1 % of stanzas — italicised in print, no

>    chronology is now first-class. Grassmann 1876–77 → Griffith 1896 → Geldner 1951–57 →
>    Elizarenkova 1989–99, and each later translator could read the earlier, so a divergence
>    between later and earlier is **not symmetric** — the later one often departs

>    precedence, so the taxonomy could not express this. Cards carry a deterministic
>    chronology band and order the renderings earliest-first. Also states what R4's rights
>    decision left implicit: Griffith 1896 is the layer's **only** English witness while the
>    standard is Jamison–Brereton 2014, so every English-side finding rests on a translation
>    **118 years** older than the standard (66 of 100 cards say so).

>    [v1.109.0](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/releases/tag/v1.109.0))** —
>    two contrast bugs, both mine and both the same mistake: v2/v3 set a `background` on
>    `mark.rv-hit` / `.rv-why` / `.rv-asym` / `.rv-chrono` and left the foreground to
>    `inherit`, giving white-on-yellow and pale-on-pale. Every coloured block now sets
>    **both**. The 10-line subtitle is one line; methodology moved to the page end.

> **H1901 🔴 EXECUTED 29-07-2026 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5[1m]`), isolated worktree** —
> spike S2 closed on three model arms after the operator supplied an
> `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. Same seeded 50-stanza sample, 300 (stanza × pair) labels
> per arm: `deepseek-chat` / `openai/gpt-4o-mini` / `google/gemini-2.5-flash`.
> Cohen's κ for `lexical_variant` vs `semantic_shift`: **0.089 / −0.012 / 0.256**
> (mean ≈ 0.11, one pair below chance) — **the fine classes are NOT separable**,
> K3 fires. Shipped in [PR #876](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/876),
> released as [v1.104.0](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/releases/tag/v1.104.0).
>
> **This REVERSES H1844's ruling above.** That entry declined to collapse the
> taxonomy because the 12,000-label pilot *used* `lexical_variant` 6.0 % of the
> time — an argument from **usage rate, which is not separability**. A class can be
> assigned often and still be assigned unreproducibly. H1844 flagged the
> separability half as unmeasured and labelled itself NOT-YET, so this completes
> rather than contradicts it, but the ruling it recorded is withdrawn.
>
> **Do not collapse blindly either:** the coarse three-class projection reaches only
> κ 0.216–0.350 — "fair", not reliable. So the five-class taxonomy is not defensible
> as a reproducible instrument and the coarse one is not yet demonstrated reliable.
> The step-8 human gate is the arbiter of that, which makes it MORE load-bearing
> than when it was cut — now mirrored as a `@DO` in
> [GTD_NEXT_ACTIONS.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/GTD_NEXT_ACTIONS.md)
> and registered in [REVIEW_SHEETS_INDEX.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/REVIEW_SHEETS_INDEX.md).
>
> **Two findings to carry:** (1) raw agreement on that subset reads 89.0 / 95.1 /
> 85.7 % and is **worthless** — all three models default to `semantic_shift`, so
> they agree by sharing a prior; under this base-rate skew percent-agreement
> measures the skew, and any gate here reporting it without a chance-corrected
> statistic is reporting the prior. (2) `added_by_one` is assigned **0 times by all
> three models** (0/300, 0/300, 0/267) despite Griffith 1896 padding freely — an
> instrument defect; the vote should decide whether to re-prompt it or drop the
> class. Operational: `google/gemini-2.0-flash-001` was a **guessed** id that 404'd
> all 50 calls at $0.00 — look ids up against OpenRouter's live model list, do not
> guess. Two new arms cost $0.054.

> **H1632 🔴 EXECUTED 26-07-2026 (Opus 5 `claude-opus-5[1m]`), isolated worktree** —
> PWG-sense × DCS attestation/frequency pilot join on the frozen H1455/H1456
> 500-headword frame:
> [research/PWG_SENSE_DCS_ATTESTATION_PILOT.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/research/PWG_SENSE_DCS_ATTESTATION_PILOT.md).
> **52/7,746 PWG leaf senses (0.67%) grounded** to a DCS attestation by shared
> locus, vs 500/500 at lemma level — the latter true *by construction*, since the
> frame was selected DCS-attested, so every rate here is an upper bound.
> Sense-taggable ceiling = 10.8% of the frame's 943,877 DCS tokens. Two ceilings
> separated: 12,953 `<ls>` sit on structural parent sense nodes (unattributable by
> PWG's own structure, before DCS is consulted), and 86.8% of groups fail
> corpus-side on texts DCS lacks / vulgate↔BORI locus drift. Deterministic, five
> inputs SHA-256 pinned, selftest green.
>
> **H1632 SCALE-UP 🔴 EXECUTED same day (MG-authorised), Opus 5.** The biased-frame
> caveat above is now resolved with real numbers. Random (n=2,000, seeded) +
> **full-PWG (109,050 groups)** frames: lemma-level attestation is **39.8%**, not
> 100% — so **60.2% of PWG has no DCS attestation at any granularity**. The sample
> estimate 40.4% (±2.2% at 95%) covers the population value, validating the frame.
> The `m_wordsem` mass ceiling is frame-invariant (10.8 / 11.9 / 11.2%), so it is a
> corpus-annotation property. Grounding did **not** scale and is reported
> **unknown, never 0%**, outside the H1455 aligner's 500 groups
> (`R0_grounding_not_computed`; selftest asserts the rate comes back `None`).
> Synthesis:
> [research/PWG_SENSE_DCS_FRAME_COMPARISON.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/research/PWG_SENSE_DCS_FRAME_COMPARISON.md).
> **Verdict — three constrictions multiply and compute fixes none of the first two:**
> 60.2% not in the corpus · 88.8% of DCS token mass untagged · only the residue is a
> locus problem. The next real lever is running the H1455 aligner over a larger frame
> **and** adding texts/locus crosswalks — not a better matcher, and not more compute.

  **Do not retry by simply reopening a harness chat** — that was the original plan and it is
  now measured wrong: `credential_status()` run *inside the interactive harness session*
  returns `auth_token_present=False`, same as in a subprocess, so `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN` is
  absent from every Python-reachable environment here (the harness lends it only to its own
  Agent tool). Wrapping the Agent tool returns assistant *text* with no per-call `usage` and
  no `total_cost_usd`, and the locked contract classes absent usage as `cost_evaluable=false`
  ⇒ an immediate NO-GO **by construction**, which is why call 1 was withheld rather than
  spent. What unblocks Wave 1, in order:
  1. **Get a metered transport** — direct HTTPS to `https://router.cheap` with a credential
     readable by Python and a `usage` block (incl. `total_cost_usd`) in the response. Inject
     it as `GatewayCall.transport`; the capture side needs no change (that is what the 10/10
     selftest already proves).
  2. **Then call 1** — smallest synthetic final-envelope check, `max_calls=1`.
  3. **Then call 2** — `dq_canary_puregloss` against the existing golden manifest
     `pwg_ru/h994/canary/dq_canary_puregloss~~h0_zz_pw.manifest.v2.json`; **never rewrite it**.
  4. **Only on PASS** — mint the gateway-w1 handoff. The `h1447-m50-w1` c4 lease is untouched
     and stays the fallback. Gateway budget window: up to $500, ~09-08 to ~16-08-2026.

  (H2361 residual, three steps, no live consumer yet).** [FINDINGS §524](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/FINDINGS.md)
  measured Griffith 1896 misaligned against its own key for ṚV. 8.49–8.103 — **73/368
  anchors match (19.8 %)** vs 92.2 % for 8.1–8.48 and 87–94 % for every other maṇḍala, so
  `lookup(lang='en')` returns a **wrong-hymn** `status='hit'` for 678/10,552 stanzas
  (6.4 %) with no signal at the call site. Nothing is broken today only because
  [`corpus_gate`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/corpus_gate.py)`._citation_reuse`
  calls `consult_card()` at the default `lang='ru'`. Order matters:

     [`citation_tm.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/citation_tm.py)
     returns a **typed miss** (not a hit, not an exception) for maṇḍala 8 hymns ≥ 49, so a
     wrong verse can never be served while the data is still wrong.

     [`rv_griffith_extract.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/rv_griffith_extract.py)
     and regenerate `pwg_ru/griffith_en_1896.json` (Aufrecht numbering vs. the appended

     `--selftest` into CI **only after** step 2; it exits non-zero today by design and

- **⭐ H2299 CLOSED 06-08-2026 — the measured-leg hang is classified, and the gate was
  spawning from the WRONG DIRECTORY** (Opus 5 1M `claude-opus-5[1m]`, zero spend, no probe
  fired). Full write-up:
  [C4_MEASURED_LEG_KILL_CEILING_HANG_CLASSIFICATION_06-08-2026.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/pwg_ru/h2299/C4_MEASURED_LEG_KILL_CEILING_HANG_CLASSIFICATION_06-08-2026.md);
  tables recomputable via
  [`h2299_series_analysis.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h2299_series_analysis.py).
  **Root cause:** `_probe_call` passed no `cwd` to `run_tree_kill` (default `None` =
  inherit), so every probe call spawned from the **repo** and paid full project-context
  injection — the tax H2158 measured at **−33 % cost / −30 % wall** and removed from the
  **paid** lane only. The gate therefore **priced a different call than the lane it gates**,
  and that ~30 % is the headroom the 03-08 / 05-08 measured legs ran out of against the
  300 s kill. Fixed to `cwd=bare_cli_cwd()` (derived from the paid lane's helper, never a
  literal), pinned by a selftest verified red-then-green.
  **Three things a future session must not redo:**
  1. **Do not re-propose an H2138 ceiling re-fit for a 0-byte kill.** `elapsed_ms` 300 099 is
     the kill constant + teardown, not a latency value; fitting a ceiling to it fits the
     ceiling to itself, and any value that "passed" it would pass a reading with no content.
  2. **Do not read the "v3 fails 2/2, v2 passes 5/5" split as causal.** v2→v3 moved only
     verdict thresholds, which cannot make a call hang; it is a date marker for the

  terminate?" was already answered by the data. **(2)** `b0` was never observed to hang: it was
  killed by **our own gate**, because `killBudgetForCur` grants any single-card batch
  `KILL_CEIL_MS` unconditionally — its budget *is* the ceiling, so it died at 180 044 ms and then
  300 073 ms (+44/+73 ms of `setTimeout` dispatch), "converging on no bound" only because the bound
  moved with it. **(3)** It is not whole-card-specific: **11 of 16 calls in the 180 s run died at
  180 0xx–180 2xx ms and only ONE was whole-card** — five of `nakzatra`'s eight heal groups and all
  three retries died on the same gate, because `killBudgetMs` clamps to CEIL above ~1.6 KB, so every
  call in these windows runs on a flat ceiling budget. **The real defect:** `_presplit_hit` routed on
  `(1+<ls>) > max(OUTPUT_BUDGET, PRESPLIT_SOLO_CITE_FLOOR)`, letting the per-**batch** packing cap
  (90) mask the per-**card** fail-solo floor (40) — inert at every default run by construction, as
  the source itself said. w1's cards score 80/79/75, above the floor and below the cap, so
  `presplit_keys` came out `[]` and all three were attempted whole and killed. The trigger now
  compares against the floor alone; presplit coverage across w1–w5 goes **10/48 → 44/48 keys**, and
  the only four left whole-card are exactly the four scoring ≤ 40 (`rAtra` 33, `spfS` 30, `idAnIm`
  36, `prasU` 31). New tooling: [`src/pilot/h2160_regen_medium50.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h2160_regen_medium50.py)
  (replays the coordinator's own `prepare`, verifies `config_dir_fingerprint` unchanged) and
  [`src/pilot/h2160_batch_shape_probe.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h2160_batch_shape_probe.py).

  `test_presplit_cite_floor_is_not_masked_by_batch_budget`), `lang_parity_check` **90 entries no
  drift** (52 re-stamped; the one functional line changed is language-blind, so every SHARED /
  INTENTIONAL-DIVERGENCE verdict stands on unchanged structural grounds). Tables:
  [RESULTS_LOG.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/RESULTS_LOG.md).

- **H823 presplit cite-floor + single-card CEIL harness fix — ✅ SHIPPED, but live verify 0/6 (12-07-2026, Opus 4.8 `claude-opus-4-8`).**
  Root-caused the H255 presplit-cohort loss: the CITATION presplit trigger `(1+<ls>) > OUTPUT_BUDGET`
  degenerates under `--output-budget=1` (any citation card force-fragmented; heal groups die on the
  slow host's ~60 s budgets). Fix: floor the cite trigger at `max(OUTPUT_BUDGET, PRESPLIT_SOLO_CITE_FLOOR=40)`
  (`--presplit-solo-cite-floor`, no-op for the default 90) so tiny cards translate whole; + give ANY
  single-card batch the CEIL (180 s), not just no-fallback singles (H220 generalization). Unit-tested
  (`test_presplit_cite_floor_and_single_ceil`); suite + lang_parity green (new `presplit_cite_floor_h823`
  SHARED). **Verified applied** (`no_pwg_presplitfix`: presplit_keys emptied; kills moved 60 s→180 s CEIL)
  **but 0/6 recovered** on the *further-degraded* host — 4 exceed even 180 s (a 298 B card >180 s vs 54 s
  ~3 h earlier; whole-card is a bigger prompt than a fragment), 2 are genuine content failures
  (`avy_ahata` wrong key1, `avyagra` dropped 2/3 `{#…#}` spans). Cohort re-classed: 4 API-hard (land on a

  **Two structural findings, both now blocking a re-tune:**
  (1) the **per-card heal cap cannot bind by construction** — `MAX_AGENTS` derives from batch count
  and is the *same counter* the heal lane spends, so when every card heals the window budget always
  binds first (3 consecutive runs, `partial_keys` empty each time);
  (2) the **warm-up gate is necessary but not sufficient** — a trivial 3.3 s probe said GO and the
  window still degraded; the probe must send a ≥5 KB skeleton-sized payload.

  — append-only log keyed on the *reading*, with `abort` rows (backfill retroactively marks runs 1–2
  NO-GO). **Do NOT re-tune the heal budget on this evidence.**

- **H220 no-PWG single-card throughput — ✅ ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED 06-07-2026 (Opus 4.8 `claude-opus-4-8`; gen Sonnet 5 `claude-sonnet-5`).**
  The no-PWG lane's ~36 % single-card yield was root-caused (fresh 10-card diagnostic window through the Workflow tool + its own `journal.jsonl` / kill-log) to **two compounding failure modes**, both fixed in [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py): **(A, dominant)** the wall-clock **kill gate** abandons valid-but-slow single supplement cards — calibrated on dense `tyaj` root batches, its byte-scaled budget (53–104 s) is below a tiny single card's fixed StructuredOutput latency (~55–105 s), and single-fragment no-PWG cards have no selfheal lane to route a kill to → permanent null (6/6 nulls were kill-timeouts, 4 of them would have passed `accept()`). Fixed: a no-fallback single now gets the **CEIL budget (180 s)** via `killBudgetForCur(cur)`; the aggressive gate stays for multi-card/splittable batches. **(B, secondary)** **key-echo mismatch** — the portrait's clean SLP1 `key1` (`CAyA`) pulls the model off the mangled `=== CARD _c_ay_a~~h0_zz_pw ===` header, so strict `km[k]` drops it. Fixed: nominal windows re-key via `nominal_keymap` when the SLP1 maps to exactly one pending stem (gated on `META.nominal`; PWG strict matching untouched). Plus observability: `selfHeal` no longer overwrites the real upstream reason with `no-selfheal-fallback`. **Verified: same 10-card window re-run → 10/10 ok (100 %, was 4/10 = 40 %), 0 kill-timeouts (was 6), 9 agents (no retries).** 3 new `window_selftest.py` tests, full suite green (83 PASS), `lang_parity_check.py` clean (new SHARED entry). Detail: [`RUN_LOG.md` H220 block](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/RUN_LOG.md). **The 232-lemma no-PWG lane is UNBLOCKED for scaling** (standard drain discipline: branches+PRs from `origin/master`, `--no-tm` on requeue, ≤3-wide, `--gen-model-version claude-sonnet-5`; heed `perf_preflight.py`; no-PWG cards are single-fragment so keep small batches).

  reads the gitignored `corpus_lexicon.jsonl` (DeepSeek L1 word/phrase units) + `corpus_strata.json`
  and emits standards-compliant TMX 1.4b: one `<tu>` per L1 unit, `srclang="sa-slp1"` (source seg = SLP1
  key, printed surface kept as `<prop type="surface">`), full provenance/grade `<prop>` spine
  (`layer/grade/modality/kind/work/passage/group/genre/period/date`). **Every unit stamped `grade=C`**
  until the composite grader (Slice 2, `src/tm_grade.py`) lands. Re-applies the never-invent guards
  (drops non-Cyrillic `ru`, empty key, `ru==sa`) at export; content-derived deterministic `tuid`s;
  `--sample N` reviewable slice; `build` self-round-trip-validates; `_audit.py` validates the release
  TMX when present. Output `release/corpus_tm/` gitignored (rights — in-copyright named RU translations;
  no release before Slice 5 clearance). Docs:
  [`src/BUILD_TMX.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/BUILD_TMX.md);
  committed fixture
  [`src/fixtures/corpus_lexicon.fixture.jsonl`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/fixtures/corpus_lexicon.fixture.jsonl).
  Validation: `python src/build_tmx.py selftest` (3/6 rows kept, escaping + props clean) + a real
  fixture `build`.

- **H215 Slice 2 — composite A/B/C TM grader — ✅ DONE 06-07-2026 (Opus 4.8, `claude-opus-4-8`;
  [PR #193](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/193)).** New
  [`src/tm_grade.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/tm_grade.py)
  assigns the publication-grade stamp from a versioned composite (qe 0.35 · source 0.30 · consensus
  0.20 · align 0.15): **source_weight** (real, [`tm_source_weights.json`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/tm_source_weights.json))
  + **consensus** (real, whole-corpus — distinct works agreeing on one `(passage,slp1)`) + **qe**
  (pluggable: deterministic reference-free `proxy` default / COMET-QE `comet` hook) + **alignment**
  (Slice-3 proxy, weighted low). `A` = composite ≥ 0.70 AND corroborated (≥2 works, ≥50%) OR
  human-adjudicated; `B` ≥ 0.55; `C` else. `build_tmx build --grades <sidecar>` stamps real grades;
  `_audit.py` runs the grader selftest. **Measured over the full 1,091,528-unit corpus (qe=proxy):
  A 5.7% / B 94.0% / C 0.3%**, all 62,503 A units consensus-gated (0 violations). Calibration on the
  320-row `gold/gold_set.jsonl`: 0 defective rows reach A, but proxy QE separates acceptable/defective
  at only AUC ≈ 0.58 (a surface heuristic can't detect wrong-sense → semantic lift is consensus +
  A-gate; `--qe comet` / Slice 3 aligner are the next steps — [FINDINGS §70](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/FINDINGS.md),
  renumbered from §60 on 11-07-2026, duplicate-key repair).
  Grades sidecar + TMX stay gitignored (rights). Docs: [`src/BUILD_TMX.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/BUILD_TMX.md).
  **Next:** Slice 3 (L0 segment layer + real SimAlign/awesome-align word-align cross-check → real
  `alignment_confidence`), Slice 4 (oral ingest, coordinate H174), Slice 5 (FAIR release — the real
  blocker: per-translator rights clearance). Handoff:
  [`H215`](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/handoffs/H215-Opus_RussianTranslation_pwg_ru_publication_grade_tm_tmx_and_oral_06.07.26.md).

- **H155 follow-up — failure-mode taxonomy + wall-clock KILL GATE — ✅ DONE 04-07-2026
  (Opus 4.8, `claude-opus-4-8`).** MG: "sense-count is just one case — what other cases are
  possible? Plan in advance. And add a gate: if a translation runs too long, kill it, don't
  wait for miracles." **(1) Taxonomy** — [`FAILURE_MODES_AND_KILL_GATE_2026-07-04.md`](FAILURE_MODES_AND_KILL_GATE_2026-07-04.md)
  enumerates every whole-card/batch StructuredOutput-stall driver from the timeline: citations
  (#1, handled), senses (#2, handled H155), **gloss-prose volume (#3), masked-token count (#4),
  multi-layer nesting (#5), batch-level sums (#6)** — all the same disease (too much output),
  surfacing via whichever attribute spikes first; any single-metric budget is blind to the
  rest. **(2) Kill gate** — runtime backstop for the un-triggered drivers: every schema-bearing
  `agent()` call is raced against a `setTimeout` budget scaled to its masked-skeleton byte
  volume (best time predictor — output ≈ 2× skeleton); overrun by `KILL_FACTOR=2×` → abandoned,
  cards routed to the bounded fragment lane instead of the full ~5-deep retry cap. Runtime
  probe: `setTimeout`/`Promise.race` work (relative timer; `Date.now()` banned), **no
  `AbortController`** so a killed call keeps running in the background until its own cap — the
  harness just stops blocking. Constants calibrated from a 13-call `tyaj --no-tm` benchmark
  (legit ≤84 s @ ~25 ms/skel-byte): BASE 30 s, SLOPE 45 ms/byte, FLOOR 120 s, CEIL 480 s →
  budgets 127 s (744 B) … 416 s (3956 B). On kill, `resolveGroup` breaks to BINARY_SPLIT and
  `healGroup` bisects, so a killed card is **recovered, never dropped**. Proven: zero-token
  behavioural test (`kill_mechanism_test.mjs` 9/9) + static wiring selftest
  `test_kill_gate_wired`; full suite green (47 tests). LANG_PARITY entry `wall_clock_kill_gate`
  (SHARED, lang-agnostic — keys on skeleton bytes, no RU/EN branching); gate clean (14 entries).
  **Discipline:** when a new stall class appears in a real run, promote it from kill-gate-caught
  to its own structural trigger (as senses did) so the next one spends zero doomed tokens.
  Files: [`src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py`](src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py),
  [`src/pilot/window_selftest.py`](src/pilot/window_selftest.py),
  [`LANG_PARITY.md`](LANG_PARITY.md), [`PIPELINE_HISTORY.md`](PIPELINE_HISTORY.md) Phase 8 #6.

- **H155 — `tyaj~~h0_zz_pw` StructuredOutput stall root-caused + FIXED — ✅ DONE 04-07-2026
  (Opus 4.8, `claude-opus-4-8`).** `tyaj`'s drain stalled ~7 min on one agent retrying the
  identical `agent()` call (`root: must have required property 'cards' / must NOT have
  additional properties`) and never producing valid output; MG stopped it. **Root cause:** the
  presplit router (built for 150-`<ls>` citation giants) prices output complexity as `1+<ls>`,
  which is **blind to SENSE density**. `tyaj~~h0_zz_pw` is a PW addenda card compressing a whole
  root article (base verb + Caus/Desid + every prefix combo) into **35 senses / 47 divs / 67
  gloss-spans** carrying only **11 `<ls>`** → weight `1+<ls>=12` ranked it among the *lightest*
  cards while its real output surface was the *heaviest* of the root, so it deterministically
  blew the whole-card StructuredOutput retry cap (the model can't emit the full card as valid
  StructuredOutput, so it never returns `{cards:[…]}`). NOT schema-broken (the schema is one
  shared const across all 5 batches; only this batch failed) and NOT stochastic (identical error
  every retry). The `_zz_pw` addenda class is a known cap-trigger (`gam~~h0_zz_pw01` hit it
  before, recovered only via selfheal after the doomed retries). **Fix** (lang-agnostic →
  SHARED): a **second, orthogonal presplit trigger** keyed on the deterministic fragment count
  (== sense-objects the model must emit) vs a new `SENSE_PRESPLIT_BUDGET=20` — routes sense-dense
  cards straight to the proven fragment lane, converting up-to-5 doomed retries + binary-split
  cascade into zero. Threshold 20 sits on a clean shelf (survey: only ~0.2% of 8,391 sampled
  cards exceed 20 senses; every known-good whole-card head is far below — sam 6, pari 8).
  Independent of the citation trigger and of byte/citation batching mode; tunable via
  `--sense-presplit-budget=N` (`off` disables). **Validated live** (fresh Workflow, store
  untouched): the `[sam, zz_pw]` pair that stalled now returns **cards:2 / ok:2 / null:0**, with
  `zz_pw` routed via presplit and healed **complete** (4 fragment groups, `partial_keys:[]`),
  6 agents / ~4 min. Pinned by `window_selftest.py::test_sense_dense_card_presplit` (synthetic
  30-sense/1-`<ls>` card; 46/46 green). LANG_PARITY entry `sense_presplit_trigger` (SHARED) +
  all drifted hashes re-affirmed → parity gate clean (13 entries). Files:
  [`src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py`](src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py),
  [`src/pilot/window_selftest.py`](src/pilot/window_selftest.py),

- **EN audit gate ported the 2026-07-03 RU gate-bug fixes — audited + 1 real fix ported,
  2026-07-04 (Sonnet 5, `claude-sonnet-5`).** Audited [`audit_window_en.py`](src/pilot/audit_window_en.py)
  against the 3 RU gate-bug fixes in [`audit_coverage.py`](src/audit_coverage.py) /
  [`prompt_rule_audit.py`](src/pilot/prompt_rule_audit.py) (see the 2026-07-03 "3 gate-bug
  fixes" entry above). Verdict per fix:
  1. **Multi-layer raw-marker over-count (`raw_markers`/`layer_sense_count`) — N/A.**
     `audit_window_en.py` never calls `audit_coverage.py` or compares raw `.raw.txt`
     sense-marker counts against card senses at all; its only coverage check is per-sense
     `MISSING-EN` (empty english when german had gloss prose). No analogous mechanism exists
     to carry the bug.
  2. **`braced_gloss_risks` Sanskrit-span leak (`SANSKRIT_SPAN` scrub) — already safe.**
     `audit_window_en.py`'s `prose()` helper already strips `{#..#}` Sanskrit citation spans
     (via `SAN.sub`) before any residue/DE-RESIDUE/RU-RESIDUE matching runs — the EN gate was
     never vulnerable to the SLP1-substring collision that hit RU's `braced_gloss_risks`.
  3. **German/Latin/French misclassification (LATIN_BINOMIAL/FRENCH_WORDS/LATIN_WORDS) —
     REAL EN analogue found and fixed.** `prompt_rule_audit.py`'s per-braced-gloss
     foreign-vs-German classification (`looks_foreign_literal`/`looks_german_gloss`) has no
     EN counterpart (EN's `MARKUP-LOSS` is a pure `{%..%}`/`<div>` occurrence count, not
     content classification), so that half of fix 2 doesn't apply. But EN's `DE-RESIDUE`
     check (`DE_WORDS` regex over the translated english prose) has the SAME collision class:
     `des` is both the German genitive/plural article ("des Todes") and a French
     partitive/plural article, and `gen_fidelity_judge_en.py`'s own judge prompt explicitly
     instructs the EN generation to preserve French/Latin literals verbatim (e.g. "de basse
     extraction", "inire feminam") — exactly the material RU's fix 2 was protecting. Verified
     the collision live (`DE_WORDS.findall('des chevaux') -> ['des']`) before fixing.
  **Fix:** extracted `LATIN_WORDS`/`FRENCH_WORDS` out of `prompt_rule_audit.py` into a new
  shared module [`foreign_literal_guards.py`](src/pilot/foreign_literal_guards.py) (imported
  by both `prompt_rule_audit.py` and `audit_window_en.py`, per the "shared helper" pattern),
  added `AMBIGUOUS_DE_FR_WORDS = {'des'}` and a `FRENCH_CONTEXT_WORDS` regex (FRENCH_WORDS
  minus `des`/`du`, so the French-confirmation check can't trivially match on the very token
  being disambiguated), and guarded `audit_sense`'s DE-RESIDUE: a bare `des` hit with no other
  unambiguous German marker, alongside a confirming French word elsewhere in the prose, is
  now suppressed as a preserved French literal rather than flagged. Pinned by
  `test_en_de_residue_french_guard` in
  [`window_selftest.py`](src/pilot/window_selftest.py) (French "des basse extraction" must
  NOT fire DE-RESIDUE; German "des Todes" alone still must). Full `window_selftest.py` run
  confirmed green on every test this change could affect (`test_en_gate_strict_has_teeth`,
  both `test_markup_loss_soft_flag_*`, `test_coverage_gate_multi_layer_and_presplit`,
  `test_en_residual_coverage_complete`, the new test) — `test_perf_preflight_small_tm_and_no_tm`
  fails on this worktree both before and after the change (`no rootmap for 'gam'`, an
  unrelated pre-existing environment/data gap, confirmed via `git stash`).
  **Also found (out of scope, fixed by a separately-spawned task):** this file
  (`.ai_state.md`) carried literal unresolved `git stash`/merge conflict markers committed
  into HEAD around the H148 vid-run entry — resolved by `task_5568538f` (already landed on

- **H130 gam Max run — Workflow-tool schema bug FOUND + FIXED, gam translated + mechanically
  audited 2026-07-03 (Sonnet 5, `claude-sonnet-5`).** Executing
  [`H130-Sonnet_RussianTranslation_pwg_ru_gam_max_run_03.07.26.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/handoffs/archive/H130-Sonnet_RussianTranslation_pwg_ru_gam_max_run_03.07.26.md)
  hit a hard blocker before any translation happened: **100% of the harness's `agent()`
  calls (61/61) were rejected pre-execution** by the CLI Workflow tool's safety classifier
  with `output schema too large to classify safely` (0 tokens spent — rejected before any
  model call). Three isolated single-agent tests pinned the exact cause: a toy 1-`$def`
  schema succeeded; the real `CARDS_SCHEMA` (5 `$defs`: card/record/sense/judge/judge_issue,
  ~4.7 KB) failed even with a trivial one-line prompt; the SAME schema with the unused
  `judge`/`judge_issue` `$defs` pruned out succeeded instantly. Root cause in
  [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py) `build()`: it read the **entire**
  `$defs` block from the shared
  [`schemas/pwg_ru_final_card.schema.json`](schemas/pwg_ru_final_card.schema.json) (which
  also carries the judge-report `$defs` for a different pipeline step) instead of only the
  subset reachable from `card` — dead weight that pushed every StructuredOutput call over
  the classifier's threshold. **Fix shipped** (branch `codex/pwg-gam-gate-fixes-v2` — the
  original `codex/pwg-performance-spend-gate` branch it was developed on hit a merge
  conflict against `master` after PR #116 landed a parallel perf-gate change; re-applied
  source-only via `/branch-contention-recover`, see the closing bullet below): new
  `_ref_names`/`_reachable_defs` helpers walk
  `$ref` pointers from `'card'` and filter `$defs` to the reachable subset before building
  `CARDS_SCHEMA`; `window_selftest.py` 40+/40+ green after the change. **This unblocks every
  root's production run through the CLI Workflow tool, not just gam** — committed and merged
  (see PR link in the closing bullet below). Finding + fix detail cross-posted to
  [`Uprava/FINDINGS.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/FINDINGS.md).
  **gam outcome after the fix:** the 4 real (non-TM) keys — `gam~~h0_01_sec_1_0` (presplit,
  13 fragment groups + binary-split retries), `gam~~h2_36_upasam`, `gam~~h3_05_ni`,
  `gam~~h3_07_vinis` — all translated clean (16 agents, ~759 K tokens, ~13 min wall-clock,
  0 failures). Merged in Python with the 123 TM-resolved cards (order-preserving —
  `window_provenance.stale_check` compares key **lists**, not sets, so the merge must
  follow `meta.selected_keys` positional order, not TM-first) into a canonical
  `wf_output.json` (127/127 accounted, 0 null). `audit_window.py --root gam --write-requeue`:
  **109/127 clean, 18 requeue** (`coverage` 13, `prompt_semantic` 6, 1 key in both);
  `nws`/`sense_dupes`/`translation` gates all PASS; 0 transient nulls. The 18 requeue keys

  **⚠️ TM-staleness-on-requeue trap discovered + fixed (same session).**
  `requeue_from_audit.py gam`'s default harness generation resolved **18/18 of the flagged
  keys straight from `translation_memory.ru.json`** (`agent_expected_after_tm: 0`) — i.e. it
  would have silently re-served the EXACT SAME already-audit-flagged content, since the
  content-addressed TM keys on input SHA, not on whether the cached translation actually
  passed the gates. Confirmed concretely: the TM entry for `gam~~h0_zz_pw03` has exactly
  **47 senses**, matching the audit's `COVERAGE-LOW(47/59)` flag byte-for-byte. Generated a
  targeted `--no-tm` harness instead (`gen_opt_harness2.py gam --keys=<18> --no-tm`) to force
  real re-translation. See [`Uprava/FINDINGS.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/Uprava/blob/main/FINDINGS.md)
  for the generalized finding — **any requeue-for-audit-failure harness must pass `--no-tm`**,
  since `gen_opt_harness2.py`'s TM lookup has no concept of "this cached translation already
  failed the gates".
  **Two requeue rounds run (both `--no-tm`, order-preserving Python merge into `wf_output.json`
  each time — `window_provenance.stale_check` compares key lists positionally, not sets):**
  round 1 (18 keys, 16 agents, ~759K tokens, ~21 min) → **109→119/127 clean, 18→8 requeue**
  (`h2_10_ava`/`h2_24_upa`/`h0_zz_pw01`-adjacent keys cleared); round 2 (the remaining 8 keys,
  14 agents, ~757K tokens, ~18 min) → **119→121/127 clean, 8→6 requeue**. **Diminishing
  returns confirmed — the SAME 6 keys failed BOTH fresh-translation attempts**:
  `gam~~h0_02_sec_2`, `gam~~h0_38_sam_a`, `gam~~h0_zz_sch` (prompt_semantic, high-confidence
  `missing_required_sense_field`-adjacent flags) and `gam~~h0_20_ava` (`COVERAGE-LOW(6/8)`),
  `gam~~h0_63_sam_0` (`COVERAGE-OVER(13/8)`, also the presplit giant, `partial:true` was even
  set on a *different* key `gam~~h0_45_upa` from round 1's presplit lane), `gam~~h0_zz_pw04`
  (`COVERAGE-OVER(8/5)`) — assessed at the time as "a genuine sense-segmentation disagreement
  between the model and the deterministic `<ls>`/sense counter... not random noise." **This
  assessment was WRONG for most of these 6 keys — a second session (same day) continued past
  this point, root-caused 3 real gate bugs, fixed them, and got gam to 127/127 clean.**

  **✅ CLOSED 2026-07-03, same day (Sonnet 5, `claude-sonnet-5`).** Concurrency note first: a
  second session picked this handoff up mid-flight and, before realizing another session
  (this one) was already running requeue round 2 live, briefly overwrote the shared
  `wf_output.json` with an incomplete duplicate. Fully recovered with no data loss by
  reconstructing the merge from both sessions' scratchpad artifacts (TM-resolved cards +
  original translate run + both requeue rounds) — logged here as a caution: **two chats can
  race on the same non-git-tracked `wf_output.json` with no lock**, and a session resuming a
  requeue handoff should re-check `window_status.md`'s timestamp against wall-clock before
  assuming a stale state is safe to overwrite.

  **Root-caused, fixed, and selftest-pinned 3 real gate bugs** (not translation defects):
  1. **`audit_coverage.py` multi-layer over-count** — `raw_markers()` used
     `t.split('===', 2)` (only 2 splits), so any sub-card bundling >1 `=== LAYER: ... ===`
     block (PW/SCH/PWKVN addenda cards, e.g. `gam~~h0_zz_pw04`: 3 layers) had its `<div n="p">`
     cross-reference continuations from layers 2+ counted as if they were numbered senses.
     `<div n="p">` is a **structural** prefix/secondary-conjugation divider elsewhere in this
     codebase ([`root_units.py`](src/root_units.py), [`scale_preflight.py`](src/scale_preflight.py)'s
     `_DIVP`, [`verify_root_glue.py`](src/verify_root_glue.py)), never a sense boundary — fixed
     by splitting into per-LAYER bodies and counting only the `〉` glyph / numbered `<div n="N">`
     per layer, floored at 1 (a pure-cross-reference layer is still one translatable unit).
     Also added a presplit/fragment-card carve-out: those cards produce one JSON row per
     fragment chunk by construction (not per raw sense), so their row count was tripping
     `COVERAGE-OVER` as a false positive (`gam~~h0_63_sam_0`: `27/8`) — now skips the
     threshold check and labels them explicitly instead.
  2. **`prompt_rule_audit.py` German/Latin misclassification, three distinct instances:**
     (a) `LATIN_BINOMIAL` (meant for species names like "Homo sapiens") lacked the
     `and not GERMAN_GLOSS_WORDS.search(...)` guard its sibling checks (`LATIN_WORDS`/
     `FRENCH_WORDS`/`ENGLISH_GLOSS_WORDS`) all have — German capitalizes every noun, so an
     ordinary "Jmd zusammenkommen" (Jmd = common B&R abbreviation for "jemand") matched the
     Capitalized-word+lowercase-word shape unconditionally; (b) `FRENCH_WORDS`' short-word
     entry `\bdu\b` collided with the German pronoun "du" in colloquial questions ("wie
     kommst du darauf?"), and `GERMAN_GLOSS_WORDS` had no short pronouns/interrogatives to
     break the tie — added `Jmd/jmdm/jmdn/du/wie/woraus/dieses`; (c) `LATIN_WORDS` didn't
     recognize `sequi, obsequi` (Latin, B&R's own untranslated-euphemism convention) — added.
  3. **`braced_gloss_risks`'s "leaked" check scanned the WHOLE Russian text including
     verbatim `{#Sanskrit#}` citation spans** — a 3-letter gloss like `{%mit%}` (correctly
     translated to `{%с%}`) coincidentally matched as a substring of an unrelated SLP1
     citation token (`miTunO` → lowercased `mituno` contains "mit"). Fixed by scrubbing
     `{#...#}` spans (new `SANSKRIT_SPAN` regex) before the substring search.
  All 3 fixes are pinned by new `window_selftest.py` cases
  (`test_coverage_gate_multi_layer_and_presplit` + 3 assertions added to
  `test_braced_gloss_audit`); full suite green after each fix.

  **After the fixes:** re-audited gam from the SAME merged `wf_output.json` (no re-translation
  needed) → **121→126/127 clean, 6→1 requeue.** Ran one more `--no-tm` requeue round on the
  last 2 non-gate-bug residuals (`h0_63_sam_0` fragment-drop, `h0_zz_sch` untranslated "die")
  — `h0_63_sam_0` got WORSE across attempts (2→3→7 missing self-heal fragments on 3
  independent tries, confirming it's a genuine API-reliability gap on this 115-`<ls>`/9-group
  presplit card, not a code bug); **recovered by using the OTHER session's original round-2
  attempt for this one key, which was actually complete (0 partial)** — a reminder that
  "latest requeue wins" is the wrong default when a retry can regress a complete card.
  `h0_zz_sch`'s hedge word (Schmidt 1928's own `"die"?`, question-marked as an uncertain
  reading) survived 2 independent fresh-translation attempts unchanged — genuinely ambiguous,
  not a miss. **Manually resolved** using an independent third attempt's own rendering
  (`«которая»?`, preserving the same hedge) rather than inventing one. **Final: 127/127
  clean, 0 requeue.** Promoted: `promote_final_cards.py --gen-model-version claude-sonnet-5`
  (2122 non-null cards / 10432 senses across the full store, not just gam),
  `audit_translation_provenance.py` clean (0 unresolved, 0 missing SHA), card TM + fragment TM
  both rebuilt (`translation_memory.ru.json` 2122 cards, `translation_memory.frag.ru.jsonl`
  +40 new fragments).

  **⚠️ Open question for MG / a follow-up session:** the 3 gate-bug fixes above were verified
  narrowly (the specific evidenced instances + a hand-built pinning fixture) but **not

  sweep over all promoted roots to see how many historical residuals silently clear.

- **Translation-memory (TM) sidecar SHIPPED + wired + live-validated (2026-07-02, Opus 4.8
  `claude-opus-4-8`; [PR #87](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/87)
  merged).** New [`src/pilot/translation_memory.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/translation_memory.py):
  a **content-addressed** cache keyed on `f"{lang}:{input_raw_sha256}"` — the address is
  computed identically at harvest (each store row's `provenance.input_raw_sha256`) and at
  generation (`sha256_file` of the `.raw.txt`), so it is exact, **version-safe** (changed
  source → new SHA → miss → re-translate; a stale translation can never be reused), and gives
  **cross-card reuse** for byte-identical sources. Built from the local-only store: **2,121
  cards / 10,841 senses / 49 roots** (4 legacy no-SHA rows skipped). Wired into
  [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py)
  as default `--tm=auto` (explicit `--no-tm` opt-out; `--tm=PATH` override): TM-hit keys go
  to a `TM_RESOLVED` lane (canonical, restored-markup cards, **no `agent()` call**) and are removed from every translate lane
  (batches / presplit / selfheal frags); total-accounting invariant preserved
  (`tm ∪ batched ∪ presplit == selected`, disjoint). `window_selftest.py` **24/24** (+2 TM
  tests). Validated: **gam 127→3** cards route to translation (124 pre-resolved); **jIv 17/17
  served from TM live via the Workflow runtime at 0 agents / 0 tokens / 70 ms**. Build/refresh
  the TM after any promotion: `python src/pilot/translation_memory.py build --lang ru`. TM json
  is gitignored (derived from the local-only store, regenerable).
  **NEXT INCREMENT (sub-card sense/fragment reuse) — ✅ SHIPPED 2026-07-02 (Opus 4.8
  `claude-opus-4-8`); see the fragment-TM bullet below. The
  [H068-Opus_RussianTranslation_pwg_tm_sense_level_02.07.26.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/archive/H068-Opus_RussianTranslation_pwg_tm_sense_level_02.07.26.md)
  it pointed at is SPENT — do not re-execute.**

- **Fragment-level TM (sub-card sense/fragment reuse) SHIPPED + wired + LIVE-VALIDATED
  (2026-07-02, Opus 4.8 `claude-opus-4-8`; branch `feat/pwg-tm-fragment-reuse`, PR pending).**
  The higher-value increment on top of the card-level TM: reuse ONE fragment inside a
  still-failing giant flat headword (kAla/ka/SrI) and the same meaning shared byte-for-byte
  across a root and its derived noun. **Approach (a) — capture ground truth at heal time,
  NOT re-align the store.** New in
  [`translation_memory.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/translation_memory.py):
  `frag_address(lang, frag_source)` = `sha256(lang + NUL 'frag' NUL + plan()-chunk)` — the
  chunk is the EXACT deterministic `autosplit_requeue.plan()` fragment **including** the
  header it attaches to fragment 0 (real translated source on both sides; the handoff's
  "strip the header" step was an artifact of the discarded store-`de` alignment and would
  have broken the round-trip — DEVIATED, documented in the module). `build_frags` harvests
  the harness's new per-fragment `frag_prov` channel from `wf_output` into an append-only,
  fsha-deduped sidecar `translation_memory.frag.<lang>.jsonl` (gitignored, regenerable);
  `load_frag_tm` reads it. Wired into
  [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py)
  under the SAME `--tm` switch: `build()` addresses each precomputed fragment and emits a
  `FRAG_TM[k]` map (mirrors `FRAGS[k]` group shape); JS `selfHeal` serves cached fragments
  with **NO `agent()` call** and heals only the uncached, emitting `frag_prov` for the
  freshly-resolved — so a partial giant card re-runs only its missing fragments, a
  fully-cached card heals at zero cost. Every fidelity guard preserved (per-fragment {Tn}
  multiset + whole-card `<ls>/{#` reassembly). `window_selftest.py` **25/25**
  (+`test_frag_tm_reuse`); `node --check` clean on empty + populated FRAG_TM. **Live proof
  (`gam~~h0_02_sec_2`, 7 fragments / 23 senses, store untouched — harvested from wf_output,
  no promotion):** run-1 cold = **5 agents / 312,393 tokens / 117 s**; harvest `build-frags`;
  run-2 `--tm` = **0 agents / 0 tokens / 31 ms**, card returned complete and **byte-identical**
  to run-1 (23/23 senses, `<ls>` 60==60). Refresh the sidecar after every heal run:
  `python src/pilot/translation_memory.py build-frags --lang ru` (stale = misses, never wrong
  reuse). The card-level TM shadows a whole-card hit first, so fragment reuse lands exactly
  where it's needed: cards never promoted whole (partial giants) + cross-card shared fragments.

- **Opt2 harness: S10 recovery machinery is now DEFAULT + presplit router SHIPPED
  (MG decision 2026-07-02, Fable 5 (`claude-fable-5`) approach-review session; this PR).**
  [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py):
  selfheal, binary-split, and citation-weighted `--output-budget=90` (raised from the

  ledger + total accounting preserved). Backward compat: an EXPLICIT `--budget=N` keeps
  byte mode (FU1's documented `--budget=6000` unchanged); `--no-selfheal` /
  `--no-binary-split` / `--output-budget=off` restore old behavior; `--selfheal` /
  `--binary-split` accepted as no-ops so all queued runbooks work verbatim.
  `window_selftest.py` 17/17 PASS, `node --check` PASS, presplit + byte-mode + defaults
  smoke-verified on vas/gam.
  **Knob calibration A/B — ✅ EXECUTED 2026-07-03 (Sonnet 5, `claude-sonnet-5`),
  [PR #101](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/101).** 4-arm live
  Workflow run in a fresh worktree off `origin/master` (branch
  `knob-calibration-20260703`), scratch-only (never touched the RU store).
  `--output-budget` 60 vs 90 on the 56-card `hA` root: **90 wins clearly** — 60 agent
  calls vs 66 (−9%), 4.03M vs 4.68M tokens (−14%), 496s vs 1,082s wall-clock (−54%),
  identical 0/56 null rate → **shipped as the new `OUTPUT_BUDGET` default.**
  `AUTOSPLIT_LS_BUDGET` 18 (stock) vs 10 (finer) on the 150-`<ls>` `gam~~h0_00_pwg00`
  giant head: finer fragmentation made things **worse** — 21 agent calls vs 13 (+62%),
  1.46M vs 925K tokens (+58%), 1,207s vs 615s wall-clock (+96%), same outcome →
  **left unchanged at 18.** Full numbers:

  finding appended to [`FINDINGS.md` §55](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/FINDINGS.md).

  - **Gate de-noise SHIPPED 2026-06-29** ([`src/pilot/prompt_rule_audit.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/prompt_rule_audit.py)).
    Investigation of the untranslated-German bucket found it was **~40 gate false
    positives + 1 real delimiter typo**, NOT translation failures — Sonnet rendered
    every simple German gloss correctly (verified on `anu`/`upa` sense 1). FPs:
    (a) B&R **Latin** euphemisms (`inire feminam`, `legere`, `inveteratus`,
    `convenire`) and **French** (Renou layer) flagged as German; (b) **Sanskrit
    preverb** braces on the SCH layer (`abhi`, `upanis`); (c) required **side-by-side
    `{%German%}`** echoes kept beside the Russian. Fixed `looks_foreign_literal`
    (Latin/French sets), `looks_german_gloss` (skip IAST/preverb tokens),
    `braced_gloss_risks` (suppress side-by-side echoes adjacent to Cyrillic; still
    flag bare echoes), `untranslated_german_residue` (strip retained
    `{%..%}`/`{#..#}`/`<ab>`/`<ls>`/`<is>` before the residue search). Re-audit:
    **clean 101→109, requeue 26→18**; `window_selftest.py` +2 fixtures, all pass.
    Remaining genuine German residual = `anu`'s one `{#`→`{%` delimiter typo +
    ~8 cards with trivial `und`/`mit`/`auch`/`für` connectives in xref/corrigendum
    tails (optional tiny Sonnet polish, low value). The
    `suspicious_attested_without_text_signal` *F-gate-nws-fp* (NWS owner citations
    in `has_text_signal()`) was a DIFFERENT FP family — **FIXED 2026-06-29** (see
    Completed below).
  - **Harness redesign PROTOTYPED + INTEGRATED 2026-06-29** (full writeup
    [`TLONLY_PROTOTYPE.md`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/TLONLY_PROTOTYPE.md)).
    Investigated the "4.2M tokens / one verb" cost concern → the dominant cost is the
    FIXED per-card subagent overhead (~27–46k tokens of system-prompt `cache_create`
    paid ONCE PER CARD), not the translation. Built the batched+masked production
    harness [`gen_opt_harness2.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/gen_opt_harness2.py):
    masks each card (reuses [`pwg_mask.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pwg_mask.py),
    whose attributed-`<ls n=...>` leak I fixed), bin-packs N cards per agent call,
    restores `{Tn}` to source markup IN-JS → canonical `wf_output.json` (audit consumes
    unchanged). **A/B on 8 mixed gam cards: \$1.72 → \$0.17 = −90% cost**, 0 leftover
    `{Tn}`, markup fidelity 8/8, quality parity. Cost parser
    [`parse_workflow_cost.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/parse_workflow_cost.py)
    reads the transcript cache split (caveat: `output_tokens` under-reports structured
    runs → estimated from tool-call size). Hardening: positional card mapping + length
    check + post-restore `<ls>`/`{#..#}` fidelity guard (nulls mismatches → requeue).
    **Open before rollout:** tune batch budget for dense roots; run a full mixed root
    end-to-end through `audit_window.py`; wire `gen_opt_harness2.py` into `RUN_FREQ_MAX.md`
    as default. THEN resume Stage C (`yuj`…) on the −90% harness.

  - **Retry blowup DIAGNOSED + FIXED 2026-06-29.** The "schema retries" were the model
    calling FILE TOOLS to list the input dir — opt2 reused the production `TR` verbatim,
    which still said "INPUTS for headword KEY (read both): KEYFILE.raw.txt / .portrait.json"
    (the original `gen_opt_harness.py` strips this; opt2 didn't), contradicting the
    masked-inline design → model read unmasked files + globbed the dir. Fix: neutralize that
    block (explicit do-NOT-open-files/list-dirs/call-tools). **yuj re-run: tool_uses 49→19,
    cost $4.96→$3.52 (−29%), `cache_rd` 1.81M→0.25M; masking integrity restored;** per-card
    ≈ −54% vs legacy. Remaining cost = per-batch prompt cache-creation (long full TR); a
    leaner masked-regime TR is the next (quality-risky) lever. gam's earlier $4.45 was
    inflated by the same bug → would improve on re-run.

    Deterministic substitution of stray lone German connectives left in the Russian
    field (`und`→`и`, `mit`→`с`, `ohne`→`без`, `auch`→`также`, phrase `fehlerhaft
    für`→`ошибочно вместо`) — applied ONLY to free text, never inside retained

    ambiguous multi-word phrases (`aber nur in der Saṃhitā`) left for review. No LLM
    tokens — Python owns this cleanup. Applied to `gam`: 8 cards, 9 subs →

    `audit_window.report.json`, `audit_window.report.md`, and `requeue.keys.txt`.
    `audit_translation.py --wf` audits workflow keys instead of the default manifest, while
    `audit_coverage.py` now treats stale/missing raw inputs as `NO-RAW`. Current stale `sTA`
    workflow output produces 20 deterministic requeue keys (old unbatched `pwg00*` labels plus
    `ud`, `ni`, `pw07`) and exits non-zero by design.
    Speed follow-up: `audit_window.py` no longer shells through `run_real_test.py audit`; it
    collects once, runs NWS in-process via `nws_split.check_result()`, and parallelizes the free
    gates. Measured current `sTA` audit: ~1.26 s vs ~8.62 s legacy path.
    Production-line follow-up: `audit_window.py` now writes `window_status.json/.md`;
    `root_window_status.py` preflights rootmaps/generated inputs/citation batches before Max
    spend; `requeue_from_audit.py` turns `requeue.keys.txt` into the exact rerun harness.
    `RUN_FREQ_MAX.md` now treats `audit_window.py` as the canonical audit path.
    Drift-proofing follow-up: `gen_opt_harness.py` now embeds workflow `meta` (root/mode,
    selected keys, rootmap SHA-256, per-input raw/portrait SHA-256) and enforces `tools: []`
    for translate agents; `audit_window.py` refuses stale artifacts before collect/gates/glue
    unless `--allow-stale` is passed, writes `window_ledger.jsonl`, and records state
    `stale_artifact` for the old 106-card `wf_output.json` against the regenerated 123-card
    `sTA` rootmap. Normal path smoke: one-card matching fixture passes collect + NWS/fidelity/
    coverage/sense-dupes + glue. Print-gate dashboard added to `preflight_remaining_gates.py`
    (`release/gate_status_snapshot.{json,md}`).
    Live dashboard follow-up: `src/pilot/dashboard_server.py` now serves a local browser page
    at `http://127.0.0.1:8765/`, refreshing every 5 seconds from `/api/status`; it combines
    Max-run status, recent audit/print-gate events, ledger entries, file freshness, and the
    G5/G6/G7/G10 print gates. `dashboard_events.jsonl` is append-only under `src/pilot/output/`;
    event writes are best-effort and do not make audits fail.

    semantic judge queue `judge_sample.keys.txt` (all Python-gate failures + default 10% clean
    sample, minimum 5 where available), records sample seed/counts in reports/status/ledger,
    and keeps this separate from the mechanical `requeue.keys.txt`. `RUN_FREQ_MAX.md` now has
    the decision-complete loop: preflight → optimized harness → Max Workflow → audit → requeue
    or sampled semantic judging. Production-line metrics follow-up: `audit_window.py` also
    accepts Max wall-clock/token/cap fields, writes them into `window_status.json/.md` and
    `window_ledger.jsonl`, and prints a `next_action` so each root status says whether to

  verify; it may only shrink; and `check`/`execute`/the receipt now read the ceiling off the

  **batches** these cards 2-per-agent-call (`batches: [["idAnIm","prasU"],["rAtra","spfS"]]`,
  `presplit_keys: []`), so it issues 2 calls per arm where the rig issues 4. Absolute
  wall-clock/token figures are therefore not production figures.

## 🧠 Dev Notes & Hypotheses

- **SOLVED 13-08-2026 — the `rc=0` zero-usage class (H2591 → H2598 B1 → H2612).** Standing
  status since 12-08-2026 was *intermittent, root cause UNPROVEN*; H2598's B1 could only
  refute the leading hypothesis (a byte-identical replay did not reproduce it, and a single
  provider limit window was refuted outright — ordinal 10 of H2591 returned `rc=0` with
  75 580 tokens *between* refusals 9 and 11, so the churn is per-call, not per-window).
  H2612's run reproduced it at ordinal 5 and the `usage_cross_check` caught it:
  `type: result` · `subtype: success` · `terminal_reason: completed` · `is_error: false` ·
  `num_turns: 2` · `total_cost_usd: 0.4029715` · a full audited card at coverage 1.0 —
  **and a top-level `usage` block of all zeros beside a `modelUsage` reporting 73 620
  tokens.** So it is not limit churn, not a failed call, not a driver misread: **the CLI can
  drop the top-level accounting block on an otherwise-clean result while `modelUsage` keeps
  the real counts.** Halting on that discards a measurement that exists, so `recover_usage`
  now adopts `modelUsage` in **that one direction only** (`usage` all-zero AND `modelUsage`
  populated), stamps the envelope `usage_source: modelUsage`, and discloses every recovered
  call in the receipt. B1 had already measured the two sources agreeing exactly on healthy
  calls, so this recovers a number rather than inventing one. **Any other disagreement**
  (both populated and different, or `usage` populated while `modelUsage` is zero) remains
  unexplained and still stops the run. Anything upstream that reads `total_cost_usd` or the
  top-level `usage` alone should assume this class exists.

- **Cost model is fragment-count-driven, not card-count-driven — the `pril10_w1` lesson (2026-07-05).**
  The opt2 harness amortizes the ~30 K fixed framework by packing many cards per `agent()` call.
  That amortization **only holds when cards are batched**. In nominal+presplit mode each fragment is
  its own agent call, so a window's true cost ≈ (agent count) × (framework re-cache + per-fragment
  I/O), and **agent count = Σ fragments, not Σ cards**. Measured: 8 cards → 230 agents → cache-write
  = 60% of a $80 bill. **Any cost/throughput estimate must be over expected *agent* count** (the
  generator already prints `agent_expected_after_tm` — surface it as tokens+$ pre-flight). Hypotheses
  to confirm in H189: (1) batch fragments per call to re-amortize; (2) share one cached system prefix
  across a window's agents if the runtime allows; (3) route `kAla`-class monster heads (349 `<ls>`)
  to a separate human-budgeted lane; (4) recalibrate the kill gate (`KILL_CEIL_MS` 8 min → well under
  3 min; > ~60 s single-fragment = suspicious, per MG). See H189.

- **Translation-memory policy questions answered (2026-07-04, MG; do not re-ask in fix plans).**
  Standing decision record: [`TRANSLATION_MEMORY_DECISIONS.md`](TRANSLATION_MEMORY_DECISIONS.md).
  Decisions: machine-gated exact reuse is acceptable for draft windows; fuzzy TM should suggest
  wording to the model only (advisory, not pre-resolving output); speed is the priority for this
  TM engineering pass; MW English may influence RU only through a curated Sanskrit-to-Russian
  terminology layer, not directly.

- **--selfheal VALIDATED LIVE (2026-07-01).** Ran the in-harness auto-split fallback on 6 marquee dense EN heads. **ji~~h0_00_pwg00 HEALED end-to-end** (whole-attempt failed → split 8 fragments → stitched **48 senses**, fidelity OK, escalated:true) — the batch-fails→heals cycle works in the wild; the compact `summary` (ok/null/healed/null_keys) reported it cleanly. Added **per-fragment retry (3×)** so one flaky fragment can't sink a multi-fragment heal. Re-run: man recovered, brU still the lone holdout (198 `<ls>`, 13 frags). **Quality insight:** a giant that passes on the WHOLE-card attempt (variance) is fidelity-valid but STRUCTURALLY COARSE — man came back 3 senses, banD 1 sense (all citations crammed in), vs ji's proper 48 via heal. So prefer the split-heal for structure even when the whole attempt would pass. Cost: ~2.2M tok/6 giants — the fragment-GROUPING tier (undone) is what makes heal economical (8 frags → dozens of calls). Efficiency levers shipped: #1 selfheal, #2 summary-return, dashboard lazy-load (13MB→6.6KB); remaining #3 binary-split / #5 output-budget = gated+validated pass.

- **Dense-card splitter TIER LADDER (2026-07-01) — the permanent fix for the recurring "dense card fails retry-cap" issue.** `src/pilot/autosplit_requeue.py` (per-card, in-place, NO rootmap churn; `_pilot_gen_merged --root-split` can't be used post-hoc — it regenerates a root's whole rootmap and orphans its translated cards). BUILT: **T0** whole-retry (tiny variance fails) · **T1** sense-split (numbered) · **T1b** sub-sense split (lettered a/b/c, greek) · **T2** citation-split (a sub-sense >LS_BUDGET `<ls>` → batches, stitched back into one sense) · completeness guard (never emit a lossy partial) · post-stitch `<ls>` fidelity check. Proven: recovered gam `sec_1_0` (117 `<ls>`) that beat every retry round; gam RU now 123/127. **UNDONE tiers (future):** (1) atom-GROUPING into budget-sized fragments — 1-atom/frag over-splits (85 calls for a 9.6KB card); (2) **sparse/degenerate cards** — near-empty cross-refs (upa 237B "— upa vgl. X") fail because there's ~no gloss to translate → schema violation; needs pass-through (ru=de, no LLM) or zero-gloss schema; (3) multi-record/homonym split (reassembly assumes records[0]); (4) **auto-escalation INTO the harness** — self-heal on accept()-null/retry-cap instead of a manual requeue step (the real end state); (5) order-robust regroup reassembly; (6) fidelity auto-repair (warns, doesn't fix); (7) `{#..#}`-density budget (T2 only counts `<ls>`).

- **⚠️ CORRECTION — gam residual did NOT need head-splitting (2026-07-01, Opus 4.8
  `claude-opus-4-8` orchestration; generation = Sonnet 5 `claude-sonnet-5`).** The Queue/handoff
  predicted gam's 23 residual cards were "truly-giant output-size-bound heads" needing
  `--root-split` / lower `HEAD_CIT_BUDGET`. **False for gam.** The main head `gam~~h0_00_pwg00`
  was already translated; the 23 residual were **prefix sub-cards** (a_bi, ud, ni, upa, pari, the
  h2 homonym set…) 0.5–5.6 KB each + one `zz_pw00` pw-supplement (10 KB). They capped on
  **citation/entity density inside a shared batch, not single-card output size**. Fix that worked:
  (1) small-batch requeue `--budget=6000` (6 batches) → 104→123; (2) **solo re-run** (`--budget=1`,
  one card/batch) of the last 4 → **127/127 = 100%, 0 null**. The batch-1 trio (a_bi/__a_0/pary_a)
  had gone null together = whole-thunk retry-cap poisoning small members, not size. **Lesson for
  viS/jan/Sru/vac/… : check whether the residual actually contains the `pwg00`/head sense-parts
  before reaching for the head-splitter; if it's prefix/supplement cards, solo re-run first.** Only
  the AB-LOSS hard flag `gam~~h0_24__a_1/scaus-4 (2/4)` remains (a step-2 item, unrelated to coverage).

- **H2636 (Opus 5, `claude-opus-5`) — H2612's fragment-lane run EXECUTED: NO-GO, 13-08-2026.**
  A human authorized the spend; the run went 16/16 with no stop and **zero evidence holes** —
  the first non-INCONCLUSIVE verdict this rig has produced
  ([PR #1671](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/pull/1671),
  [v1.144.42](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/releases/tag/v1.144.42),
  [RUN_RESULT.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/pilot/h1210/h2612/RUN_RESULT.md)).
  On the lane carrying 92 % of cards, PREP context does **not** earn a route change at n=7
  paired units (paired wall +4.25 %, tokens +3.34 %, neither clearing the pre-registered
  10 %) — and does not hurt either: no audited card was lost to it.
  **Three driver defects the run surfaced, each now pinned by a test:**
  1. **The GO rule fired twice on an artefact of arm TOTALS.** The receipt said GO on
     +10.21 %; remove arm A's single refusal and the same margin inverts to **−8.85 %**,
     while the honest paired figure is +4.25 %. An arm total silently rewards the arm that
     fails *faster*. `build_receipt` now keys the verdict off `paired_deltas` (units where
     BOTH arms returned schema) and labels the totals as not the basis. The original GO
     receipt is kept unmodified as the evidence for the change, with the re-grade beside it.
  2. **H2591's unexplained `rc=0` zero-usage class is IDENTIFIED** — see Dev Notes.
  3. **A timeout could never classify as one**: `parse_error` was checked before `timed_out`,
     and a timed-out call returns empty stdout that never parses, so every abandoned call was
     filed `malformed_envelope`. Attempt 1 died exactly there; a probe then returned the
     byte-identical prompt in **307 s**, proving the 1800 s ceiling was never the problem.
  Spend: **23 paid calls** over three attempts + one diagnostic probe outside any ledger,
  `unknown_gateway` throughout, no store/TM/promotion/default-route write. Matrix 22/22 at
  merge, window suite 211/211. **H2595 was retired the same day** — it was filled from
  H2591's withdrawn GO, and with both lanes now measured there is no descriptive GO to scale.

- **H2361 Griffith 1896 EN misalignment against its own key, ṚV. 8.49–8.103 — 🔴 EXECUTED

  merged, released in [v1.144.20](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/CHANGELOG.md);
  registry row closed 09-08-2026.** The EN of-record feed
  `pwg_ru/griffith_en_1896.json` (10,552/10,552 loci, one of the five H1844 feeds) is
  **structurally complete and semantically shifted**: the vālakhilya block (8.49–8.59
  appended rather than interleaved in Aufrecht's numbering) slides the English column
  against the key it is filed under, so `8.49.1` returns Griffith's `8.60.1`, `8.92.1`
  returns `8.103.1`, and `8.103.1` returns `8.59.1`. Measured by a **language-independent
  deity-stem anchor audit** — no translation judgement involved: 8.49–8.103 = **73/368
  (19.8 %)**, 8.1–8.48 = 436/473 (92.2 %), maṇḍalas 1–7/9/10 = 3809/4152 (87–94 %).
  Blast radius **678/10,552 stanzas (6.4 %)**, all in `lookup(lang='en')`, which returns
  `status='hit'` — the wrong verse arrives labelled as a success.
  **No consumer has been hit:** `corpus_gate._citation_reuse` calls `consult_card()` at
  the default `lang='ru'`, and the RU lane is unaffected. Deliverables: report
  [GRIFFITH_EN_RV_MANDALA8_VALAKHILYA_MISALIGNMENT_07-08-2026.md](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/pwg_ru/h2361/GRIFFITH_EN_RV_MANDALA8_VALAKHILYA_MISALIGNMENT_07-08-2026.md),
  instrument [`audit_griffith_en_alignment.py`](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/RussianTranslation/src/audit_griffith_en_alignment.py)
  (`--selftest` exits **non-zero today by design**; SKIP-exits 0 without
  `SAMUDRA_CORPUS_DB`; deliberately **not** wired into CI yet), finding
  [FINDINGS §524](https://github.com/gasyoun/SanskritLexicography/blob/master/FINDINGS.md).
  Two lessons that generalise past this feed: **a row key proves the row exists, not that
  every column in it belongs to that row** — maṇḍala 8's verse counts matched 103/103, so
  the structural check passed while the content was wrong; and **a char-count assertion
  cannot tell the right passage from a wrong one of similar length.** The fix is queued
  above as a three-step ordered residual (refuse → re-extract → gate), not done here.

  and exposes `cache_write_rate(ttl)` / `split_cache_creation()` / `usage_cost(…,
  unknown_ttl=…)`; `tally()` emits `cost` **and** `cost_unknown_at_1h`. **Asymmetric
  fallback on purpose:** reporting stays 5 m for TTL-less legacy envelopes (history frozen,
  incl. the $79.83 golden window), cost gates pass `unknown_ttl='1h'` and fail closed.
  Pinned by `h809_selftest.test_cache_write_is_ttl_priced_and_reconciles_with_the_vendor`,
  which also asserts the OLD arithmetic still fails to reconcile — a revert to a TTL-blind
